# CH5_VERIFY_TABLE — independent verification of Chapter 5, the Chapter 4 examples and Appendices A/E

Built 2026-09-25 from the verifier outputs of workflow `wf_040cbc40-2cf` (one independent verifier per part, told to re-open every raw source on both sides) and from the lab-PC md5 comparison run in the main session. Every row is one check. Verdicts: MATCH; MISMATCH (value); SETTING-WRONG (layer, bank, dataset set, pooling, probe, protocol); SOURCE-WRONG; NO-SOURCE (print-only / log-only); STYLE; PITFALL (superseded or mislabelled number); PAGE-WRONG; BOTH-SIDES-MISSING; LAB-CACHE (rests on a lab-only store, read from the cache). Every non-MATCH row was fixed in the text unless its "fix" column says otherwise.

**Unresolved:**
- The lab-PC path and md5 of the leak-guarded matrix (C:/Users/user4/expLB-resplit/..., cached at audit/cache/labpc/expLB-resplit/mean_L40_s42.json) were not checked against the lab PC. The printed text uses only git-tracked values, so nothing printed rests on it.
- audit/scripts/phase3/w4_null_lab.py wrote C:/Users/user4/audit_scratch/w4_nulls.json with LAYERS [20, 40, 44], and no local copy exists. If it holds a layer-20 HP-KR null, the new sentence 'Neither pipeline stored a random-direction null at layer 20' still holds for the two pipelines, but an audit null at L20 could be cited instead. Check it on the lab PC.
- KD11 L20, L22 and L44 (root) and run-crossgen/kd11/banks/kd/matrices/mean_L{20,40,44}_s42.json are git-tracked and clean. No lab-cache copy exists to md5 them against the lab PC in the final session.
- 'Chapter~4' and 'Chapter~6' are hard-coded, because chapter_5.tex and chapter_9.tex have no chapter \label. The writer's ch4_revision_items already propose adding \label{ch:method}.
- check_latex.py still reports 16 unresolved \refs. All are skeleton labels owned by other parts (sec:r-selection, sec:r-failed, sec:r-organisms, sec:r-analysis, sec:r-adjust, sec:r-stats, sec:r-compare, sec:r-discuss, sec:r-limits, sec:r-hpkr, sec:r-interp, sec:r-supervised, tab:r-oracle) and resolve on merge.
- The added sentence 'Pipeline A's layer-40 value lies inside their range (Section~\ref{sec:r-stats})' relies on W6a reporting the HP-KR null (plan row 9.5: 0.6436 vs p95 0.9377). If W6a drops that row, this \ref has nothing to point to.

**Verifier notes:** Everything was verified against raw sources in this session: collaborator CSVs and code via git show 16def2de, the owner's kd11 JSONs (git-tracked, clean, L40 md5-identical to the lab cache), results/subsets/harm-pressure-knowledge-report.csv (recounted with the pipeline's own denial regex), and the Liars' Bench PDF pp. 8, 10 and 54-56 (pypdf).

Most of the writer's numbers reproduce exactly: 0.9358, 0.8283, 0.109, 0.3808, 0.148/0.593/0.589, 0.2504/0.2029/0.6436/0.6032, 0.9085/TPR 0, 13.09%/10.64%/0%/2.22%, 0.3004/0.4273/0.6590/0.6984, 0.3787/0.3551/0.6327/0.5413, 9 of 24 cells at 0.0630-0.1171, 0.9399/0.0601, 124/15, and all the LB table cells. The writer was right to supersede the plan's '7 cells' (F-124, read at ea1ce52b) with 9 of 24 at the pin.

Fixes applied (only these):
(1) 'averaged over the benchmark's four models it is 0.42' was wrong. Gemma has no HP-KR entry in Table 7, so 0.42 is the mean over three models.
(2) 'base probe shows the gap at its widest' was false. The sycophancy contrast on IT has a wider AUROC-BA gap: 0.9852/0.5047 at L40, 0.9868/0.5 at L44.
(3) 'every number in this section uses mean pooling' was false for the benchmark's Follow-Up and Upper-Bound numbers, which are single-token reads. It is now scoped to the pipelines.
(4) The Upper-Bound Probe is not 'trained on HP-KR's own examples' alone (it trains on five datasets plus the control), and it is scored like the Follow-Up Probe (p.8).
(5) The five-dataset means 0.682/0.634 are ours, not printed. I added that and a pp. 54-56 citation, and 'the published average' became 'that average'.
(6) Both sides: Pipeline A's kd-bank matrices (tracked) give 0.1482/0.5936/0.5913. That is within 0.002 of Pipeline B's 8 v 7, so the A/B difference is the bank, not the pooled tokens.
(7) PITFALLS 0.4 / DF-72: the zero-shot inversion paragraph compared only with 0.5. I added that the L40 value is inside the random null (run-coords/zeroshot_nulls.json p 0.6254) and that neither pipeline stored an L20 null.
(8) KD11's HP-KR row and column means include the text-matched slice cell without the leak guard that Chapter 4 describes. I added a disclosure with the git-verifiable nine-dataset means (0.2708/0.4092 vs 0.6559/0.6932). The guarded rerun agrees (comment only). The conclusion is unchanged.
(9) Minor: 'differ only' became 'apart from cross-validation noise'; L44 is now labelled post hoc in the 8-arm sentence and the new sentences; an interp marker was added to the 0.3808 sentence; and the dataset-set sentence now covers both pipelines.

No figures or tables are in W1. No new bib entries are needed; kretschmar2025liars is in references.bib (check_latex.py found no missing keys). The hazard rule is respected. Attribution is correct: the kd bank is credited through the design, and Pipeline B's diagnostics through the pipeline.

**Unresolved:**
- The 21-layer and last-pooling sweep of the kd-role difference in Pipeline A (plan row 3.5, F-98/F-99) is still a \todo. Only mean L20/L40/L44 of run-crossgen/kd11/banks/collab/matrices are cached locally; the other 39 files are lab-only. Re-read them on the lab PC in session 6. F-99 gives a range down to +0.0019 at L0, so any sentence covering all layers must not say 'every layer'.
- Possible overlap with W1 (sec:r-problem), which may print the same numbers and define the same terms. The plan puts rows 1.1-1.6 in 5.1.3 and rows 1.1-1.5 again in 5.2.1. W2 defines 'Liars' Bench average' and 'Upper-Bound Probe' and prints 0.9832/0.7320, 0.0598, 0.7784/0.6255 and 0.682/0.634/0.93. At assembly, keep each definition in one place only and replace duplicate numbers with a \ref.
- Pipeline A's kd11 grid (0.6623, 0.6651, 0.5849, 0.5555) uses the unguarded kd11 split (chapter_5.tex:459 verified comment). 2 of its 110 off-diagonal cells are the leaked HP-KR <-> text-matched-slice pair. Without them, L40 raw is 0.6582 and datapca 0.6634, so the conclusions are unchanged. The first Chapter 5 use (W1, row 1.3) or Chapter 4 should say the main grid is unguarded.
- Pooled-probe comparison, both sides (for W6b, sec:r-pooled). In Pipeline A, the pooled LODO beats D9's 11-dataset zero-shot 0.7996: cache run-premise/premise_L40_mean_s42.json summary.pooled_lodo 0.85448 (md5 e5ddaaa1...), but that comparison is leaky. The leave-one-cluster-out version is below D9: mean pooled_loco 0.7894 in cache run-premise/clustered/clustered_L40_mean_s42.json (md5 524c1340...). The two LODO figures also disagree (0.8545 in the premise summary vs 0.8454 as the mean of the clustered file's per_dataset). W2 now scopes its claim to Pipeline B; W6b must report the direction and reconcile the two LODO values.
- The plan's row 3.6 ('the pooled probe wins CG, IT, ID and RepE') is wrong, and THESIS_BRIEF.md:168 repeats it. In loo_bootstrap.csv, delta = pooled - ext_axis1, and the pooled probe is ahead only on CG. The owner should correct the plan and the brief; the verifier did not edit them.
- thesis_guide/PITFALLS.md §C says 'The Upper Bound runs at 8%', which is only the Llama-70B HP-KR cell. The Llama-70B FPR average over the five datasets is 0.18, with HP-C at 0.50 (LB p.56). The same scoping is needed in F-124 §4's wording; propose a DF entry.
- L36 headline: depth.csv stores 0.913, and the text writes 0.9130, following the plan. The owner can choose 0.913 to match the source exactly.

The writer's numbers all reproduce. That includes the full 54-cell table, which I recomputed independently, and the 16-layer counts (11/16, -0.0325 at L68, +0.0633 at L56, 6 sign changes). The writer also correctly overrode three plan errors: the L20 diagonal (0.9564, not 0.979), row 3.6 (pooled ahead only on CG) and the 8% UB FPR (it averages 0.18).

Fixes applied (21 replacements; working file scratchpad/v2/w2_corrected.tex, written by scratchpad/v2/apply_fixes.py):
- Two factual errors: 'only layers 20 and 40 of them [the 13 remote layers] are pre-registered', and 'published values include the IT scratchpad' (true only for the Mean Probe; p.69).
- One wrong-at-one-layer claim: the D6 HP-KR 'below chance at all three layers'. At L44 its row-bootstrap interval, 0.4293-0.5309, includes 0.5.
- Scope fixes: 'at every layer', stated for the six-dataset lead (which fails at L28) and for D9's weakest dataset; the unscoped headline (now 'at layer 40'); raw vs datapca@5 (at L20 raw is ahead); the base probe on HP-KR (the claim holds at L40 only); and 'every layer measured'.
- The zero-shot table footnote had a meaningless 'seed 42' and did not name the bank.
- An overreaching interp on random3: Pipeline A's datapca also transfers.
- Both-sides additions: a sentence giving Pipeline A's sycophancy contrast on HP-KR (0.0448/0.1647/0.4587, agreeing with Pipeline B); a token-set clause on the cross-pipeline D9 comparison; and 'in Pipeline~B' on the pooled-probe conclusion.

Style fixes: an ambiguous 'all but layers 20 and 40' clause, 'every contrast' changed to 'all three contrasts', 'above chance' changed to 'above 0.5', and a \ref to sec:m-metrics for 'main metric'.

Checks that pass: attribution (kd roles ours; the regrouping, pooled LOO and 16-layer sweep Pipeline B's), the hazard rule and the labels. Substantive fixes applied:
(1) Opening sentence. Knowledge alone does not separate full HP-KR, because 1,862 of 2,000 honest rows know too. It fixes the label only on the text-matched slice.
(2) 'the sweep does not record the realised FPR' was false. The per-layer zero_shot_operating.csv files at all 16 layers hold specificity. D8's balanced accuracy above 0.5 at layers 20-36 comes from 43-97.5% realised FPR. D8's FPR is below 10% at 10 of the 16 layers.
(3) Text-matched slice at L40: 8 of 12 directions, not just the extended contrast, have row-bootstrap intervals above 0.5 (5 of 12 at L44). The strongest are paper16's deception/sycophancy/unified directions, not the kd ones.
(4) The HP-KR -> text-matched guard effect was reported at one layer only. At L20 the guard raises axes 0.6004 -> 0.7190, and the guarded cells rest on 59 rows.
(5) The passA11 store behind +0.0655 / P 0.1611 / p 0.38 is unguarded. Its design string has no leak-guard clause; the text now says so.
(6) PITFALLS rule 4. At L40 mean pooling, the full-HP-KR zero-shot readings 0.9344, 0.8765 and 0.6692 all sit inside the HP-KR random-direction null (p95 0.9377, same rows). The text now says the evidence is the fall to the text-matched slice.
(7) Three both-sides additions: Pipeline B's extended contrast on the re-extracted bank (0.8053/0.8762/0.8486, within 0.0005 of Pipeline A, so the gap is the bank); Pipeline B's 4v6 on that bank (0.8734/0.8826/0.8734, D-03); Pipeline B's raw-probe HP-KR diagonal (0.9458, 5-fold CV).

Smaller fixes: 'four of Pipeline B's six detectors'; the p.69 source comment; the paper16 bank named 'at score 3' in (b); the GS directions named as the kd bank at score 3 (commit 148ddeb9, md5-matched in Ch4:596); 'AUROC' and '(post hoc)' added where missing; '4 v 3' spacing; 'one seed' on the kd grid; two missing src comments; the guarded lab path.

What reproduced exactly: all Pipeline B operating and depth values; all Liars' Bench table values (pp.8, 54-56, 69); all Pipeline A zero-shot and trained values; the factor-probe corpus counts; every Gender-Secret value and interval; the layer-significance and cluster-permutation results. The writer was right against the plan in several places: 15/16 not 16/16; 0.9741/0.9678/0.9623 not 0.9663; 0.7756; 88.35%; 24 of 90. Hazard rule respected: no WMDP question is quoted. Attribution is correct: kd roles ours; 4v6/12v13, Gender-Secret and the 16-layer sweep Pipeline B's. check_latex on a scratch copy of the corrected text passes braces, environments, bib keys and images. Its only failures are refs to other parts' skeleton labels.

(1) BOTH SIDES. 'It was never repeated at layer 20 on the full corpus' is false. The lab own100 store (cache audit/cache/labpc/run-crossgen/banks/own100/summary.json) covers all 21 layers on the ten-dataset corpus. At L20 (mean, paper, seed 42), pc:deception reads 0.5587 (paper16) vs 0.5450 (own100), both below raw 0.6346, and axes_resid reads 0.5812 vs 0.5426. The paragraph and its interp are rewritten: paper16 stays ahead everywhere, the gap shrinks to about 0.01, and both fall below raw.
(2) tab:r-designs D6 (0.663) and D7 (0.690) all-six transfer are sign artifacts, the same mechanism as D5 at L20. A footnote now gives the sign-restored values 0.8080 and 0.8295. The conclusions are unchanged: D9 is still the highest.
(3) The 1-d = zero-shot agreement and 'the published contrast at layer 20 is the exception' are scoped to the table's arms. The syc, uni and pc1 arms are also inverted, and F-124 misses uni_axis1.
(4) The regroup reference is the released 'assistant' role, not default_vector.pt. Text and comment are corrected.
(5) The unified-basis confound also reaches paper16's own assistant direction (cos 0.767, the writer's own number). The sentence claiming 'only' PCA features is corrected.
(6) 0.896 is console-only, so a \todo is added. The '20 questions' now cite run_paper16_shared_chain.ps1.
(7) Smaller wording fixes: random3 is averaged over five draws; 'Off the diagonal' for the constant panel; 'Every other design scored at the threshold'; 'peaks at layer 40'; D9/D8 lowest-value wording; 'Section describes'; the hf275 footnote says the values are measured against the published axis; own100 'saved no result file' is replaced with an accurate statement; the strict-bank transfer comparison now names own100 and paper16_shared.

- The three HP-KR growth rates (5/20 = 0.250, 3/20 = 0.150, 5/12 = 0.417) and the GATE FAILED / MARGINAL outcome lines are log-only and lab-only; re-read C:/Users/user4/expLB/logs/hpkr_denial_{smoke,control,margin}.log on the lab PC (the audit extract gives lines 210/208/551; audit/phase1/fmap_all.csv gives 419/415/1101, a conflict to settle there).
- md5 of every lab cache file above must be checked against the lab PC. The passA, passP and scale_70b_paper16fp32 caches carry meta.json md5s that match the local bytes. The three collab-bank matrices have no meta.json.
- D3 and D10 numbers rest only on report.pdf (no result file on any of the 14 refs). DANN rests only on the owner's statement of 2026-09-25.
- Bib key aisi2026liedetection is not in references.bib. The writer's entry needs the owner's approval; its year comes from the Hugging Face 'updated 25 Jun 2026', and the creation date was not checked.
- check_latex.py flags 15 refs to other parts' skeleton labels (sec:r-selection, -problem, -hpkr, -d4, -cmp-replication, -interp, -zeroshot, -scale, -cap, -sarcasm, -pooled). These resolve only once W1-W7 are merged.
- Cross-part consistency: the 70B paragraph now uses the scale ladder's 'best' definition (max over axes, pc:deception, pc:unified, pc:unified@2; mean-pooling 70B paper16 = -0.0116, i.e. F-121's -0.012). W6b (sec:r-scale) must not quote the mixed-store F-100/F-101 values (+0.0070/+0.0079/+0.075) and should use the same definition, or the chapter will show two different 70B numbers.
- Ledger/DOC_FIXES items for the owner (not written, per the task's write limits): (a) F-120's artifact reading fails on the store's full 10 datasets (random falls 0.5718 -> 0.5498 while axes rises 0.4846 -> 0.6656), which goes beyond F-128; (b) DF-63's 'significance.json stores no margin field' is wrong (top-level margin 0.02); (c) DF-105's '17 methods not recorded' is resolved (the q6_layers.json rows with non-null gap_20_22); (d) the report's D10 claim 'PCA beats INLP at every rank' is contradicted at rank 1 by its own Figure 5.4.

**Verifier notes:** I verified W5 independently from raw files: collaborator files through git show at 16def2de (extracted to scratchpad/verW5/c/), owner files from the asix working tree, lab stores from the named caches, and report.pdf pages through pypdf text plus the p.48 figure image. The corrected LaTeX is at C:/Users/SURAIYA/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-SURAIYA-Documents-STUDY-thesis-expLB/61942ac4-b75f-408f-b4af-ba1bd297edf5/scratchpad/verW5/W5_corrected.tex (md5 b4461a1f). It is reproducible from the writer's copy (verW5/W5_orig.tex, md5 d1cf6c75) with verW5/apply_fixes.py, which applies 32 exact single-occurrence replacements. I wrote nothing in the repository. I ran fig_widening.py as a scratch copy with its output redirected.

Substantive fixes:
(1) 70B pooling paragraph, SOURCE-WRONG. The writer's +0.0070 (11/21) came from run-crossgen/scale_70b, whose layer 40 is the paper16 bank and whose other 20 layers are the kd bank. I checked this by zero-shot fingerprints: L20 and L44 equal kd11/banks/kd exactly, and L40 matches the paper16 root to within 0.0003. The bank was not named in the text. The like-for-like paper16 store is cached for both poolings, so the todo is resolved. Mean pooling gives -0.0116 (9/21 layers ahead of raw), last-token gives +0.0100 (14/21), and against datapca@3 it leads by +0.0605 / +0.0712. I used the scale ladder's 'best' definition so that the mean-pooling value equals F-121's 70B -0.012.
(2) Layer cost 0.0117 -> 0.0116 (the writer re-rounded after rounding).
(3) Late-layer random gain +0.1272 -> +0.1271. Five of the comment's 5-dp values were also wrong.
(4) HP-KR growth gate. 0.70 was the prediction and pass level and 0.40 the floor, so the margin route (0.417) was MARGINAL, not failed.
(5) 'top-10% has the highest recall at L20' is false: the pre-reply read has 0.4149. The sentence is reworded to compare only the two peak reads.
(6) D10: the report's 'PCA beats INLP at every rank' is contradicted by its own Figure 5.4 at rank 1.
(7) PITFALLS: the 0.409 deception contrast on sycophancy_mt is 'below 0.5', not 'below chance', because it sits inside the random null.
(8) The +-0.04 source line numbers are 762-764, not 740-741.
(9) The timing of the kd roles is now sourced on the owner side (kd/docs/DESIGN.md, commit 5b43a7af) before the collaborator's text.
(10) Smaller fixes: a referent ('its' -> 'the axes feature's'), 'Except where last-token pooling is named', the '4 v 3' style, the dataset and setting names, a definition of 'peak reads' at first use, and the DANN description. One sentence is added to 'Our own notes' on the mixed-bank F-101 reading.

Everything else reproduces exactly:
- all 54 table values;
- the fig_widening printed values;
- the collab_fig_depth.pdf bytes (21,059 = git cat-file -s; md5 identical);
- the 16-layer depth.csv values;
- the ext-vs-pc3 13/16 count with exceptions at 64/68/72;
- the claims-list recomputations (per-column matrices, base-probe inversions, syc 15/16, base 8/12 with the four trailing pairs, 0.0257 and -0.0108);
- Q2, Q6 and Q8;
- passA and passP;
- the inverted-axis 7/21 and 18/21.

Attribution is correct: kd roles ours; the 4 v 6 and 12 v 13 groupings, the 16-layer sweep and the pooled LOO runs are Pipeline B's. The hazard rule is kept (no WMDP text). check_latex passes braces and environments. The page lengths of the added sentences are small.

Fixes applied (29 replacements plus 3 follow-ups):
- Settings:
  - the blocks hold 9 to 11 datasets (heading and interp);
  - bank, judge filter and dataset set named for the five persona-vs-raw clearing tests;
  - 'the pooled set includes Alpaca' for 0.8810 (25_zeroshot_nulls.py:101-113; 12_dotproduct_layers.py drops Alpaca and gives 0.8630 — DF-65/O-087);
  - post-hoc layers 28, 32 and 44 labelled in the 23-test family;
  - 'six datasets' and 'AUROC' for Pipeline B's gap.
- Mismatches:
  - the BH family in run-coords/significance.json is the 19 diff tests, not all 23;
  - the caption claimed a row interval for every test, but the 8 Q8 tests have none;
  - 'random projection of the same dimension' is wrong for 'axes - random' (4-d vs k=5).
- Balance:
  - added the nine/ten-dataset block of the matched control (+0.0855 / +0.0811, neither significant) and scoped the interp to all ten/eleven;
  - added PC1 (label AUROC 0.2959, 25.68% of the variance) beside PC2/PC3.
- Interp: softened the unsupported dataset-identity mechanism.
- Style:
  - 'crossgen' replaced with 'train-one-test-all';
  - 'large' replaced with a stated threshold (>40 tokens; ai_liar +22.4 is next);
  - the lab src format normalised;
  - two missing % src added;
  - comment digit corrections;
  - one 'src: same' chain repaired after an insertion.

Both sides:
- Pipeline B has no dataset bootstrap, no BH, no zero-shot null, no length control and no datapca (git grep @16def2de).
- Pipeline A has no specificity.
- The generalisation gap is given for both pipelines in separate sentences, with the protocol difference stated.
- Attribution is correct.
- Hazard rule respected.

The copied co-author figure matches git cat-file -s (25,704) and the blob's md5.

Nothing is MISMATCH or PAGE-WRONG. Attribution follows the design (pooled LODO baseline = Pipeline B, commit 6b16a46; row-matched arm and LOCO = Pipeline A; kd roles ours; 4v6/12v13 and the 16-layer sweep Pipeline B's). The ledger correction chain (F-108, F-110, F-121, F-123..F-128) and PITFALLS were checked: the text uses +0.1254 not +0.1782, attaches the five seeds to LODO, gives the 70B kd value as a direction, states both banks decline, flags the last-pooling non-monotonicity and the judge-filter mix, and quotes no withdrawn number. - main.tex does not \input appendix_A yet and defines no \appendix, \chapter or \label{app:prompts}; the file's header comment says it does. Integration is the plan's step 5 and belongs to the main session.
- The rendering-equivalence check (an empty system turn renders the same as no system turn) used the laptop's HF cache snapshot 6f6073b4 of Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct's tokenizer_config.json. The tokenizer snapshot the lab PC used through nnsight was not checked (no ssh by rule).

**Verifier notes:** Verdict: the file is accurate. Every count, commit, line number, quotation, page citation and run-time claim reproduces from raw data. All 260 variants, 47 descriptions and 92 questions were checked, not a sample, and I checked the rollouts' conversation turns too, which the generator does not. Four fixes are needed, all in the generator; corrected_latex is empty as the W9 task says.

Fixes:
1. MISMATCH (line 23): the intro claims every prompt of the banks of sec:m-banks, but own100 (in tab:m-banks) is not printed. Scope the sentence and add one sentence on own100.
2. PITFALL (line 527): Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct's chat template renders an empty system turn exactly as no system turn, so the cap anchor's empty variant gives the model the same input as kd's.
   - Reword the sentence so it does not read as a third deviation.
   - The writer's Ch4 revision item 4 should keep only the literal-{model_name} half (20 cap rows). The 'empty system message' half is not a deviation in model input.
3. STYLE (line 701): 'arm' is undefined and not in the glossary. Define it at first use and report it as a glossary addition.
4. STYLE: lab-only source comments on lines 44, 45, 526 and 707 need the 'lab PC ... (cache ...)' form; line 44 gives no path.

Both sides:
- Pipeline B @16def2de has no persona prompt or question files.
- Its tab_roster.tex matches the kd groups in membership, not in order. The text says only 'same groups', so it is correct.

Checks on the writer's Ch4 revision items:
- Items 1, 2, 3 and 5 are confirmed:
  - pp. 9--10, not 9--11 (p.11 is the unified-axis rewordings);
  - the anchor has 5 variants, only the first empty;
  - the anchor also answered the 10 deception questions;
  - cap_role_groups.json :: question_set still describes the earlier 'judgement questions'.
- Item 4 holds only in part (see fix 2).
- A further Ch4 item the writer missed: chapter_5.tex:315 cites gen_multiturn_ndif.py :: 100-104 for the two-turn, teacher-forced layout. Those lines are argparse (--control, --max_new_tokens, --temperature, --batch). The layout is at 148-152, with the prompt at 158.
- Minor: the writer calls 'definition files' Chapter 4 wording. It is not in chapter_5.tex or GLOSSARY.md, but it is plain English and needs no change.

Repo safety: I did not run the generator in place; a copy wrote to the scratchpad. The only git commands I used were show, ls-tree, log, diff and rev-parse. Nothing in the repo was written.
